# Commands · P · part 4

1,427 entries, perm-deny to pillar-audit. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **perm-deny**: Persistently deny a granular permission rule ([jerrikneri/dotfiles](https://github.com/jerrikneri/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.config/opencode/commands/perm-deny.md))
- **perm-edit**: Open the user's Yes-Claude config file for editing ([rahulr85r/yes-claude](https://github.com/rahulr85r/yes-claude/blob/HEAD/commands/perm-edit.md))
- **perm-mode**: Switch the Yes-Claude plugin to a preset (allow-everything, allow-most, allow-cautious, ask) or to the example template ([rahulr85r/yes-claude](https://github.com/rahulr85r/yes-claude/blob/HEAD/commands/perm-mode.md))
- **perm-status**: Show the active Yes-Claude config and (optionally) preview what would happen for a sample tool call ([rahulr85r/yes-claude](https://github.com/rahulr85r/yes-claude/blob/HEAD/commands/perm-status.md))
- **perma-brief**: Daily morning brief — current state across all active Permanence project streams ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-brief.md) · ★1)
- **perma-consolidate**: Read-only consolidation pass over Permanence — analyse every stream and write a report of proposed changes. Changes nothing on disk or in git; a separate interactive review (/perma-consolidate-review) applies what you accept. ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-consolidate.md) · ★1)
- **perma-consolidate-review**: Interactive walkthrough of a Permanence consolidation report — decide each item one at a time (yes / no / with-notes), see a final summary to catch mistakes + dependencies, then apply the accepted ones on `main` via a single audit branch. ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-consolidate-review.md) · ★1)
- **perma-contents**: Regenerate Permanence's `CONTENTS.md` inventory files. Argument: `$ARGUMENTS` — `local` (default), `full`, or a stream path like `home/kitchen`. ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-contents.md) · ★1)
- **perma-emit**: Emit a Permanence event from THIS project to other open sessions — a semi-real-time note that something interesting happened here, delivered into other projects' context (never their files). Pub/sub: the emitter never sees its own event echoed back. ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-emit.md) · ★1)
- **perma-help**: What this Permanence can do — every installed /perma-* command, and which background machinery is actually switched on for this machine. Trigger phrases: "what can Permanence do", "Permanence help". ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-help.md) · ★1)
- **perma-list**: List every registered Permanence stream — name, what it is, when it was last touched, and its real-world path if it has one. Read-only. Trigger phrases: "list my projects", "list permanence projects", "what's registered in Permanence". ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-list.md) · ★1)
- **perma-orchestrate**: Generative orchestration pass over Permanence — detect cross-stream convergence, entangle, and propose emergent ideas into the _meta/emergent.md ledger. Divergent counterpart to /perma-consolidate; proposes only, never edits a stream. ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-orchestrate.md) · ★1)
- **perma-register**: Register an existing project as a Permanence stream — read its README, create the stream (seeded from the README), and add it to the registry. Use when the owner says "register this project", "register `&lt;path&gt;`", or passes a path to a README. ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-register.md) · ★1)
- **perma-register-group**: Register a programme group — several repos that report into one shared plan-and-status folder. Adds the GROUPS.md rows and scaffolds the task doc + folder into the target repo. Use when the owner says "register a group", "these repos are one programme", or "set up a programme folder". ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-register-group.md) · ★1)
- **perma-shutdown**: End-of-day wind-down — capture where things stand into this project's Permanence stream (PROJECT + QUESTIONS) and hand back a Done / in-flight / first-move-tomorrow summary, so the open loops leave your head instead of coming home with you. Optional argument: a Permanence stream/project name, OR an absolute path to a registered project — either works, for sessions with no meaningful working folder (desktop AI apps). Trigger phrases: "good night Permanence", "night Permanence". ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-shutdown.md) · ★1)
- **perma-startup**: Morning bookend to /perma-shutdown — project-specific "where you left off" plus a small (2-3) set of next-task options for the current repo's Permanence stream. Optional argument: a Permanence stream/project name, OR an absolute path to a registered project — either works, for sessions with no meaningful working folder (desktop AI apps) or to switch projects mid-session. Trigger phrases: "good morning Permanence", "morning Permanence". ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-startup.md) · ★1)
- **perma-unregister**: Remove a registered Permanence stream — deletes its folder (including gitignored derived files like CONTENTS.md) and its _meta/REGISTRY.md row in one step, so nothing is left orphaned. Destructive; always confirms first. Use when the owner says "unregister this project", "remove the &lt;name&gt; stream", or "delete the &lt;name&gt; project from Permanence". ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-unregister.md) · ★1)
- **perma-upgrade**: Upgrade Permanence's machinery to the latest template release — shows the plan, negotiates any customization conflicts, proposes (never auto-applies) any stream-affecting migration notes, then applies on your say-so. One command, always interactive — there's no unattended/scheduled variant. ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-upgrade.md) · ★1)
- **permacat-deploy**: Verifica o deploy do PermaCat (nup-sentinel-manifest) no Railway ([nuptechs/nup-sentinel-manifest](https://github.com/nuptechs/nup-sentinel-manifest/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/permacat-deploy.md))
- **permalink**: 指定されたファイルのGitHubパーマリンクを生成してください： ([rysk-tanaka/dotfiles](https://github.com/rysk-tanaka/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/permalink.md))

- **permission-audit**: Audit lib/repositories/ for PermissionValidationMixin compliance. For each repository class, verifies (a) the mixin is applied via 'with PermissionValidationMixin', and (b) every public, non-getter method body invokes at least one validate* call from the mixin (validateOwnership, validateWritePermission, validateSelfOperation, validateRequiredFields, hasReadAccess, getDocumentWithPermissionCheck). Outputs a markdown report at tasks/permission-audit-&lt;YYYY-MM-DD&gt;.md grouped by severity. ([Malingisslen/butlery](https://github.com/Malingisslen/butlery/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/permission-audit.md) · ★1)

- **pf-cross-review-iterate**: Wrap /pf-cross-review V3.3 in an iterative auto-stabilization loop. - **pharn-loop-close**: Part of /pharn-loop: the stop procedure it reads when a run stops. Not run on its own; use /pharn-loop. ([pharn-dev/pharn-oss](https://github.com/pharn-dev/pharn-oss/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pharn-loop-close.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **pharn-loop-quick**: Part of /pharn-loop: the --quick deltas it reads at entry. Not run on its own; use /pharn-loop --quick. ([pharn-dev/pharn-oss](https://github.com/pharn-dev/pharn-oss/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pharn-loop-quick.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **pharn-memory-promote**: Promote one lesson or pattern into pharn/memory-bank/ when the user asks to keep it, with checked provenance; nothing is written until the user accepts the rendered entry. ([pharn-dev/pharn-oss](https://github.com/pharn-dev/pharn-oss/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pharn-memory-promote.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **pharn-plan**: Turn an Approved, unchanged SPEC.md into PLAN.md with its declared files and applied_lessons declaration (plus AC-TESTS.md for a templated SPEC). Run after the SPEC is approved, before /pharn-grill. ([pharn-dev/pharn-oss](https://github.com/pharn-dev/pharn-oss/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pharn-plan.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **pharn-regress**: Check for regressions outside the feature: run the project's gates at the base commit and at HEAD and compare them (regression-report.json, REGRESSION.md). Run after /pharn-build. ([pharn-dev/pharn-oss](https://github.com/pharn-dev/pharn-oss/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pharn-regress.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **pharn-review**: Review code with PHARN's review lenses run in parallel, then merge their findings into one findings.json and REVIEW.md. Standalone, not a pipeline stage. ([pharn-dev/pharn-oss](https://github.com/pharn-dev/pharn-oss/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pharn-review.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **pharn-ship**: Run the whole pipeline for one feature when the user asks to ship it (spec, plan, grill, test, build, regress, verify), stopping at two human gates: SPEC approval and the merge/fix/abandon decision. `--quick` for a small change. ([pharn-dev/pharn-oss](https://github.com/pharn-dev/pharn-oss/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pharn-ship.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **pharn-ship-close**: Part of /pharn-ship: the GATE-2 and STOP procedure it reads when a run ends. Not run on its own; use /pharn-ship. ([pharn-dev/pharn-oss](https://github.com/pharn-dev/pharn-oss/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pharn-ship-close.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **pharn-ship-quick**: Part of /pharn-ship: the --quick deltas it reads at entry. Not run on its own; use /pharn-ship --quick. ([pharn-dev/pharn-oss](https://github.com/pharn-dev/pharn-oss/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pharn-ship-quick.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **pharn-ship-stages**: Part of /pharn-ship: the stages it runs after the SPEC is approved, one section each. Not run on its own; use /pharn-ship. ([pharn-dev/pharn-oss](https://github.com/pharn-dev/pharn-oss/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pharn-ship-stages.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **pharn-spec**: Turn a feature idea into SPEC.md: surface gaps, fill the SPEC template, stop for human approval (or, with --model-approve for /pharn-loop, the model approves), then pin it. - **pharn-supervise**: Decide an escalated full /pharn-loop run: proceed-at-risk, stop or (once) amend-spec, one reason per escalated grill finding, in SUPERVISOR.json, checked by supervisor.mjs. - **phonology-examples-check**: `phonology-examples check` queues an advisory check of one saved Example Set revision against the grammar in the project's current [Baseline](term:baseline). For each example it asks PanGloss to generate surface forms from the underlying form in the example's declared context and reports whether the expected form was observed. A check never changes the FieldWorks project, never changes Readiness, ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/phonology-examples-check.md) · ★1)
- **phonology-examples-list**: `phonology-examples list` reads the current revision of each saved phonology Example Set from the project's Motif store. Example Sets are advisory evidence and do not affect Readiness or Apply. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/phonology-examples-list.md) · ★1)
- **phonology-examples-results**: `phonology-examples results` reads the stored result of one phonology check: a page of per-example summaries, or one complete example. A check is advisory evidence about a saved Example Set against a [Baseline](term:baseline); it never changes Readiness and Apply ignores it. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/phonology-examples-results.md) · ★1)
- **phonology-examples-save**: `phonology-examples save` records underlying forms, expected surface forms and the grammar context for each example as an immutable revision, with an optional citation on each. A saved set is advisory evidence: it never changes Readiness and Apply ignores it. Saving does not change the FieldWorks project. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/phonology-examples-save.md) · ★1)
- **phonology-examples-show**: `phonology-examples show` reads one exact saved revision and returns its examples in authored order, including source context and any citations. Example Sets are advisory evidence and do not affect Readiness or Apply. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/phonology-examples-show.md) · ★1)
